FROM kalilinux/kali-rolling

WORKDIR /app

# Layer 1: Core languages that definitely exist in Kali repos
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends \
    python3 python3-pip python3-venv \
    gcc g++ gfortran \
    ffmpeg \
    r-base \
    curl wget git jq \
    nmap sqlmap hydra john netcat-traditional \
    dnsutils whois traceroute tcpdump \
    openssh-client openssl \
    vim nano less file unzip zip \
    procps net-tools iproute2 iputils-ping \
    nodejs npm \
    ruby \
    golang \
    rustc cargo \
    default-jdk default-jre \
    php-cli \
    lua5.4 \
    tcl \
    fp-compiler \
    ghc \
    gnat \
    erlang-base \
    elixir \
    ocaml \
    gnucobol4 \
    zsh fish \
    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

# Layer 1b: Install Julia via official binary (not in Kali apt)
RUN curl -sL https://julialang-s3.julialang.org/bin/linux/x64/1.10/julia-1.10.4-linux-x86_64.tar.gz | tar xz -C /opt && \
    ln -s /opt/julia-1.10.4/bin/julia /usr/local/bin/julia && \
    apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends execstack && \
    execstack -c /opt/julia-1.10.4/lib/julia/libopenlibm.so 2>/dev/null || true && \
    rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

# Layer 2: Install Kotlin, Clojure, Swift via SDKMAN/curl (not in Kali apt)
RUN curl -s "https://get.sdkman.io" | bash && \
    bash -c "source ~/.sdkman/bin/sdkman-init.sh && sdk install kotlin 2.0.0 && sdk install clojure 1.12.0" || true

# Layer 3: Install Dart via official apt repo
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends gnupg2 && \
    curl -s https://dl-ssl.google.com/linux/linux_signing_key.pub | gpg --dearmor -o /usr/share/keyrings/dart.gpg && \
    echo "deb [signed-by=/usr/share/keyrings/dart.gpg] https://storage.googleapis.com/download.dartlang.org/linux/debian stable main" > /etc/apt/sources.list.d/dart.list && \
    apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends dart && \
    rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/* || true

# Create venv for Python packages
RUN python3 -m venv /opt/venv
ENV PATH="/opt/venv/bin:$PATH"

COPY requirements.txt .
RUN pip install --no-cache-dir -r requirements.txt

COPY app.py ./
COPY src/ ./src/
COPY frontend/ ./frontend/

RUN mkdir -p /data/receipts /data/artifacts /data/audio /app/data/receipts /app/data/artifacts /app/data/audio

ENV PORT=7860
ENV PROVIDER=huggingface
ENV HF_TEXT_MODEL=Qwen/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct
EXPOSE 7860

CMD ["uvicorn", "app:app", "--host", "0.0.0.0", "--port", "7860"]

README.md:
```
---
title: CSC Engine
emoji: 🎥
colorFrom: blue
colorTo: purple
sdk: docker
pinned: false
---

# Continuity Sensory Code Engine (CSC)

A Hugging Face-hosted WebRTC/WebSocket system that turns iPhone/webcam video,
motion, user speech, background audio, diarized speakers, and retrieved context
into a rolling compressed state from which LLMs generate auditable code patches
in real time.

## Core Formula

```
Code_t = LLM_build(LLM_observe(Compress(V_frames + A_diarized + M_motion + R_retrieved)))
```

## Safety Gate

```
EmitPatch_t = 1[QVD_t > θ] · 1[permission] · 1[sandbox] · 1[attribution] · Code_t
```

## Architecture

```
iPhone Continuity Camera / webcam
→ browser WebSocket stream
→ HF Space ingest
→ frame sampler + audio segmenter
→ vision encoder + ASR + diarization
→ state compressor
→ LLM pair (observer + builder)
→ code patch stream + receipts
```

## Dual LLM

- **Observer (LLM-A)**: compresses sensory state into scene/intent/uncertainty
- **Builder (LLM-B)**: generates code patches from compressed state

## Five Loops

1. Capture: adaptive 1-4 FPS
2. Audio: 1-3s chunks with VAD + diarization
3. Novelty: skip if state unchanged
4. Compression: all streams → one compact state
5. Code: emit patch only when QVD > threshold

## Four-Pane UI

1. Camera stream
2. Live audio/speaker ledger
3. Observer reasoning state
4. Generated code patch stream

```

app.py:
```
"""
CSC Engine — Continuity Sensory Code Engine
FastAPI app for Hugging Face Docker Space.

camera + audio + speech → compress → observer LLM → builder LLM → code patches + receipts
"""

import os
import time
import json
import hashlib
import subprocess
import tempfile
import base64
import uuid
import signal
import threading
import shutil
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from collections import deque

from fastapi import FastAPI, WebSocket, WebSocketDisconnect, UploadFile, File, Body, Request
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from fastapi.responses import FileResponse, JSONResponse, HTMLResponse
from pathlib import Path

from src.stream_state import SessionState, FrameEvidence, AudioChunk
from src.vision_sampler import process_frame, should_increase_fps
from src.audio_diarizer import process_transcript, assign_speaker
from src.novelty import compute_novelty, should_observe, compute_qvd
from src.compressor import compress_state
from src.gate import builder_gate, evidence_gate, log_gate_decision, get_gate_log
from src.observer_llm import observe
from src.builder_llm import build, debug_fix
from src.code_receipts import save_receipt, list_receipts, get_receipt
from src.consensus import observer_consensus, builder_consensus

APP_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
FRONTEND_INDEX = APP_ROOT / "frontend" / "index.html"
# Use HF persistent storage at /data if available, fallback to app dir
PERSISTENT_ROOT = Path("/data") if Path("/data").exists() and Path("/data").is_dir() else APP_ROOT / "data"
DATA_DIR = PERSISTENT_ROOT
ARTIFACT_DIR = DATA_DIR / "artifacts"
AUDIO_DIR = DATA_DIR / "audio"
RECEIPT_STORAGE_DIR = DATA_DIR / "receipts"
ARTIFACT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
AUDIO_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
RECEIPT_STORAGE_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

# Terminal hardening config
EXEC_AUTH_TOKEN = os.getenv("EXEC_AUTH_TOKEN", "")  # Set via HF Space secret to enable auth
_RATE_LIMIT_STATE: dict[str, list] = {}  # ip -> [timestamps]
RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW = 60  # seconds
RATE_LIMIT_MAX = 30  # max requests per window per IP

BLOCKED_COMMANDS = [
    "rm -rf /", "rm -rf ~", "rm -rf *", "mkfs", "dd if=", "chmod 777 /",
    "shutdown", "reboot", "halt", "init 0", "kill -9 1",
    "> /dev/sda", "> /dev/sdb", "curl | sh", "curl | bash", "wget | sh", "wget | bash",
    "nc -l", "nc -lp", "python -c 'import os; os.system",
]

app = FastAPI(
    title="CSC Engine — Continuity Sensory Code Engine",
    version="1.0.0",
    description="Live multimodal code compiler: camera + audio + speech → observer LLM → builder LLM → code patches",
)

app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=["*"],
    allow_credentials=True,
    allow_methods=["*"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)


# ─── Hardening helpers ───

def _client_ip(request: Request) -> str:
    return request.client.host if request.client else "unknown"


def _rate_limited(ip: str) -> bool:
    now = time.time()
    if ip not in _RATE_LIMIT_STATE:
        _RATE_LIMIT_STATE[ip] = []
    _RATE_LIMIT_STATE[ip] = [t for t in _RATE_LIMIT_STATE[ip] if now - t < RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW]
    if len(_RATE_LIMIT_STATE[ip]) >= RATE_LIMIT_MAX:
        return True
    _RATE_LIMIT_STATE[ip].append(now)
    return False


def _check_exec_auth(request: Request) -> bool:
    """If EXEC_AUTH_TOKEN is set, require it as Bearer token or X-Auth-Token header."""
    if not EXEC_AUTH_TOKEN:
        return True  # No token configured = open access (for dev)
    bearer = request.headers.get("authorization", "")
    if bearer.startswith("Bearer ") and bearer[7:] == EXEC_AUTH_TOKEN:
        return True
    x_token = request.headers.get("x-auth-token", "")
    if x_token == EXEC_AUTH_TOKEN:
        return True
    return False


def _is_command_blocked(command: str) -> bool:
    cmd_lower = command.lower().strip()
    for blocked in BLOCKED_COMMANDS:
        if blocked in cmd_lower:
            return True
    return False


# Active sessions
sessions: dict[str, SessionState] = {}


@app.get("/")
def index():
    return FileResponse(str(FRONTEND_INDEX))


@app.get("/diary")
def diary():
    """Lab diary page — notebook-style view of all artifacts."""
    return FileResponse(str(APP_ROOT / "frontend" / "diary.html"))


def save_artifact_disk(patch_hash: str, data: dict):
    """Persist artifact to disk."""
    path = ARTIFACT_DIR / f"{patch_hash}.json"
    path.write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=2, default=str))


def load_artifact_disk(patch_hash: str) -> dict:
    """Load artifact from disk."""
    path = ARTIFACT_DIR / f"{patch_hash}.json"
    if not path.exists():
        return {}
    return json.loads(path.read_text())


def list_artifacts_disk(limit: int = 50) -> list:
    """List recent artifacts from disk."""
    files = sorted(ARTIFACT_DIR.glob("*.json"), key=lambda f: f.stat().st_mtime, reverse=True)
    artifacts = []
    for f in files[:limit]:
        artifacts.append(json.loads(f.read_text()))
    return artifacts


@app.get("/health")
def health():
    return {
        "ok": True,
        "app": "csc-engine",
        "version": "1.0.0",
        "provider": os.getenv("PROVIDER", "groq"),
        "sessions_active": len(sessions),
        "time": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
    }


@app.post("/session/start")
async def session_start():
    state = SessionState()
    sessions[state.session_id] = state
    return {"session_id": state.session_id, "created_at": state.created_at}


@app.post("/session/stop")
async def session_stop(request: Request):
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    session_id = body.get("session_id", "") or request.query_params.get("session_id", "")
    if session_id in sessions:
        state = sessions.pop(session_id)
        return {"stopped": True, "session_id": session_id, "receipt_count": state.receipt_count}
    return JSONResponse({"error": "session not found"}, 404)


@app.get("/state/current")
async def get_current_state(session_id: str):
    if session_id not in sessions:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "session not found"}, 404)
    return sessions[session_id].to_dict()


@app.get("/receipts")
async def get_receipts(limit: int = 50):
    return {"receipts": list_receipts(limit=limit)}


@app.get("/receipts/{receipt_id}")
async def get_receipt_by_id(receipt_id: str):
    r = get_receipt(receipt_id)
    if not r:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "receipt not found"}, 404)
    return r


@app.post("/generate/patch")
async def generate_patch(request: Request):
    """Force a code generation cycle regardless of QVD threshold."""
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    session_id = body.get("session_id", "") or request.query_params.get("session_id", "")
    if session_id not in sessions:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "session not found"}, 404)

    state = sessions[session_id]
    compact = compress_state(state)

    # ─── Evidence Gate: Sensory Proprietary Compiler V1 — Fallback Ladder ───
    # Gate NEVER blocks. It assigns a fallback level (1-5) and artifact_type.
    ev_result = evidence_gate("", compact)
    log_gate_decision("evidence_gate", ev_result, patch_hash="")

    # Observe with the gate result — observer gets context about what level we're at
    observation = observe(state, compact)
    state.observer_state = observation
    state.last_observer_ts = time.time()

    # Re-check Evidence Gate with observer output now available
    ev_result2 = evidence_gate(observation.get("observer_output", ""), compact)
    log_gate_decision("evidence_gate", ev_result2, patch_hash="")

    # Build with fallback level — always produces an artifact
    result = build(state, observation, gate_result=ev_result2)
    state.current_artifact = result["patch_output"]
    state.last_patch_hash = result["patch_hash"]
    state.last_builder_ts = time.time()
    state.receipt_count += 1

    save_receipt(result["receipt"])

    # Auto-store artifact so patch_hash can be resolved and executed
    patch_output = result["patch_output"]
    extracted_code = extract_code_from_output(patch_output)
    if not extracted_code:
        extracted_code = patch_output  # fallback: store raw output as code
    artifact_data = {
        "patch_hash": result["patch_hash"],
        "code": extracted_code,
        "observer_output": patch_output,
        "timestamp": time.time(),
        "session_id": session_id,
        "fallback_level": result.get("fallback_level", 0),
        "artifact_type": result.get("artifact_type", ""),
        "sensory_channels": ev_result2.sensory_channels,
        "feature_attribution": ev_result2.feature_attribution,
    }
    artifact_store[result["patch_hash"]] = artifact_data
    save_artifact_disk(result["patch_hash"], artifact_data)

    return {
        **result,
        "gate": {
            "fallback_level": ev_result2.fallback_level,
            "artifact_type": ev_result2.artifact_type,
            "sensory_channels": ev_result2.sensory_channels,
            "feature_attribution": ev_result2.feature_attribution,
            "reason": ev_result2.reason,
        },
    }


@app.post("/audio/generate")
async def audio_generate(request: Request):
    """Generate code from an audio transcript without requiring an existing session.
    Creates a temporary session, injects the transcript and audio features as evidence,
    runs observer → builder → receipt."""
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    transcript = body.get("transcript", "") or request.query_params.get("transcript", "")
    audio_file = body.get("audio_file", "") or request.query_params.get("audio_file", "")
    audio_features = body.get("audio_features", {}) or {}
    mode = body.get("mode", "continuous_code") or request.query_params.get("mode", "continuous_code")
    if not transcript:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "transcript required"}, 400)

    # Create a temporary session
    state = SessionState()
    state.mode = mode
    sessions[state.session_id] = state

    # Inject transcript as an audio chunk
    chunk = process_transcript(transcript, state)
    state.add_audio_chunk(chunk)

    # Compress and run the full pipeline
    compact = compress_state(state)
    # Inject audio features into compact state for the builder
    if audio_features:
        compact["audio_features"] = audio_features
    ev_result = evidence_gate("", compact)
    log_gate_decision("evidence_gate", ev_result, patch_hash="")
    observation = observe(state, compact)
    state.observer_state = observation
    state.last_observer_ts = time.time()
    ev_result2 = evidence_gate(observation.get("observer_output", ""), compact)
    log_gate_decision("evidence_gate", ev_result2, patch_hash="")
    result = build(state, observation, gate_result=ev_result2)
    state.current_artifact = result["patch_output"]
    state.last_patch_hash = result["patch_hash"]
    state.last_builder_ts = time.time()
    state.receipt_count += 1
    save_receipt(result["receipt"])

    patch_output = result["patch_output"]
    extracted_code = extract_code_from_output(patch_output)
    if not extracted_code:
        extracted_code = patch_output
    artifact_data = {
        "patch_hash": result["patch_hash"],
        "code": extracted_code,
        "observer_output": patch_output,
        "timestamp": time.time(),
        "session_id": state.session_id,
        "fallback_level": result.get("fallback_level", 0),
        "artifact_type": result.get("artifact_type", ""),
        "sensory_channels": ev_result2.sensory_channels,
        "feature_attribution": ev_result2.feature_attribution,
        "audio_file": audio_file,
        "transcript": transcript,
        "audio_features": audio_features,
    }
    artifact_store[result["patch_hash"]] = artifact_data
    save_artifact_disk(result["patch_hash"], artifact_data)

    return {
        **result,
        "session_id": state.session_id,
        "gate": {
            "fallback_level": ev_result2.fallback_level,
            "artifact_type": ev_result2.artifact_type,
            "sensory_channels": ev_result2.sensory_channels,
            "feature_attribution": ev_result2.feature_attribution,
            "reason": ev_result2.reason,
        },
    }


# In-memory artifact store: patch_hash -> {code, output, timestamp}
artifact_store: dict[str, dict] = {}


@app.post("/test/artifact")
async def test_artifact(request: Request):
    """Create a test artifact without LLM. For smoke testing runCode(patch_hash) flow."""
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    code = body.get("code", "print('test artifact ok')")
    patch_hash = hashlib.sha256(f"test_{time.time()}".encode()).hexdigest()
    artifact_data = {
        "patch_hash": patch_hash,
        "code": code,
        "observer_output": f"TEST ARTIFACT\nCODE:\n```python\n{code}\n```",
        "timestamp": time.time(),
        "session_id": "test",
        "test": True,
    }
    artifact_store[patch_hash] = artifact_data
    save_artifact_disk(patch_hash, artifact_data)
    return {
        "patch_hash": patch_hash,
        "code": code,
        "stored": True,
        "message": "Test artifact created. Use this patch_hash with /run or /api/artifacts/{patch_hash}",
    }


def extract_code_from_output(output: str) -> str:
    """Extract Python code from LLM output that uses **CODE**: or CODE: headers with fenced blocks."""
    import re
    if not output:
        return ""
    # Match **CODE**: or CODE: (with optional markdown bold)
    match = re.search(r'\*{0,2}CODE\*{0,2}:?\s*([\s\S]*?)(?=\n\s*\*{0,2}(?:REASONING|SCENE|INFERRED_INTENT|INTENT|SIGNALS|CANDIDATE_TASK|UNCERTAINTY|EVIDENCE|RUN|TEST|ATTRIBUTION)\*{0,2}:|\n\s*(?:INSUFFICIENT)|$)', output, re.IGNORECASE)
    if not match:
        return ""
    raw = match.group(1).strip()
    # Extract from fenced block
    fence = re.search(r'```(?:python)?\s*([\s\S]*?)```', raw)
    if fence:
        return fence.group(1).strip()
    # If no fence, check if raw looks like Python (starts with import, def, class, etc.)
    first_line = raw.strip().split('\n')[0].strip()
    if first_line.startswith(('import ', 'from ', 'def ', 'class ', 'print(', 'if ', 'for ', 'while ', '#', '"""', "'''")):
        return raw
    return ""


@app.post("/run")
async def run_code(request: Request):
    """Execute Python code in a subprocess and return stdout/stderr.
    Accepts JSON body {code, patch_hash} or query params ?code=...&patch_hash=..."""
    # Hardening: rate limit + auth
    ip = _client_ip(request)
    if _rate_limited(ip):
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Rate limit exceeded", "retry_after": RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW}, 429)
    if not _check_exec_auth(request):
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Unauthorized — auth token required"}, 401)

    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    code = body.get("code", "") or request.query_params.get("code", "")
    patch_hash = body.get("patch_hash", "") or request.query_params.get("patch_hash", "")
    if patch_hash and not code and patch_hash in artifact_store:
        code = artifact_store[patch_hash].get("code", "")
    elif patch_hash and not code:
        disk = load_artifact_disk(patch_hash)
        code = disk.get("code", "")

    # Fallback: extract code from observer_output if stored code is garbage
    if not code.strip() and patch_hash:
        disk = load_artifact_disk(patch_hash)
        code = extract_code_from_output(disk.get("observer_output", ""))
    elif not code.strip():
        code = extract_code_from_output("")

    if not code.strip():
        return JSONResponse({"error": "No code provided"}, 400)

    # ─── Builder Gate: validate before execution ───
    gate_result = builder_gate(code)
    log_gate_decision("builder_gate", gate_result, patch_hash=patch_hash, code_preview=code)
    if not gate_result.passed:
        return JSONResponse({
            "error": "Code rejected by Builder Gate",
            "gate_reason": gate_result.reason,
            "gate_checks": gate_result.checks,
            "exit_code": -2,
        }, 422)

    code = gate_result.code_extracted or code

    with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(mode="w", suffix=".py", delete=False, dir="/tmp") as f:
        f.write(code)
        f.flush()
        tmp_path = f.name

    try:
        proc = subprocess.run(
            ["python3", tmp_path],
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
            timeout=15,
        )
        stdout = proc.stdout
        stderr = proc.stderr
        exit_code = proc.returncode
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        stdout = ""
        stderr = "Execution timed out (15s limit)"
        exit_code = -1
    except Exception as e:
        stdout = ""
        stderr = str(e)
        exit_code = -1
    finally:
        os.unlink(tmp_path)

    result = {
        "stdout": stdout[-4000:],
        "stderr": stderr[-4000:],
        "exit_code": exit_code,
        "timestamp": time.time(),
    }

    # Store in memory + disk
    if patch_hash:
        if patch_hash not in artifact_store:
            artifact_store[patch_hash] = {"code": code}
        artifact_store[patch_hash].update(result)
        # Persist to disk for diary
        disk_data = load_artifact_disk(patch_hash)
        disk_data.update({"code": code, "patch_hash": patch_hash})
        disk_data.update(result)
        save_artifact_disk(patch_hash, disk_data)

    return result


@app.post("/debug")
async def debug_code(request: Request):
    """Send failed code + error output to LLM for correction.
    Accepts JSON body {patch_hash, code, stderr} or query params."""
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    patch_hash = body.get("patch_hash", "") or request.query_params.get("patch_hash", "")
    code = body.get("code", "") or request.query_params.get("code", "")
    stderr = body.get("stderr", "") or request.query_params.get("stderr", "")
    # Load from disk if not provided
    if patch_hash and not code:
        disk = load_artifact_disk(patch_hash)
        code = disk.get("code", "")
        stderr = stderr or disk.get("stderr", "")
        observer_context = disk.get("observer_output", "")
    else:
        observer_context = ""
        if patch_hash:
            disk = load_artifact_disk(patch_hash)
            observer_context = disk.get("observer_output", "")

    if not code.strip():
        return JSONResponse({"error": "No code to debug"}, 400)
    if not stderr.strip():
        stderr = "Code execution failed with no stderr output."

    try:
        result = debug_fix(code, stderr, observer_context)
        # Store the debug fix
        new_hash = result["patch_hash"]
        artifact_store[new_hash] = {
            "code": result["patch_output"],
            "debug_of": patch_hash,
            "timestamp": time.time(),
        }
        save_artifact_disk(new_hash, {
            "code": result["patch_output"],
            "patch_hash": new_hash,
            "debug_of": patch_hash,
            "observer_output": observer_context,
            "timestamp": time.time(),
        })
        return result
    except Exception as e:
        return JSONResponse({"error": str(e)}, 500)


@app.get("/artifact/{patch_hash}", response_class=HTMLResponse)
async def view_artifact(patch_hash: str):
    """Return an HTML page showing code + output, embeddable as iframe."""
    info = artifact_store.get(patch_hash) or load_artifact_disk(patch_hash)
    code = info.get("code", "")
    stdout = info.get("stdout", "")
    stderr = info.get("stderr", "")
    exit_code = info.get("exit_code", "")

    import html as html_mod
    code_html = html_mod.escape(code)
    stdout_html = html_mod.escape(stdout)
    stderr_html = html_mod.escape(stderr)

    return f"""<!doctype html>
<html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1">
<title>Artifact {patch_hash[:12]}</title>
<style>
  body {{ font-family: 'SF Mono', monospace; background: #060810; color: #e2e8f0; margin: 0; padding: 16px; }}
  h2 {{ font-size: 12px; color: #00d4ff; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 1px; margin: 16px 0 8px; }}
  pre {{ background: #0b0e18; border: 1px solid #1a1f2e; border-radius: 8px; padding: 12px; overflow-x: auto; font-size: 12px; line-height: 1.6; }}
  .code {{ color: #c8d3f5; }}
  .stdout {{ color: #34d399; }}
  .stderr {{ color: #f87171; }}
  .meta {{ font-size: 11px; color: #6b7280; margin-bottom: 8px; }}
  .btn {{ display: inline-block; padding: 8px 16px; border-radius: 6px; font-size: 12px; font-weight: 600; cursor: pointer; border: none; margin: 4px 4px 0 0; text-decoration: none; }}
  .btn-run {{ background: rgba(52,211,153,.15); color: #34d399; border: 1px solid rgba(52,211,153,.3); }}
  .btn-debug {{ background: rgba(251,191,36,.15); color: #fbbf24; border: 1px solid rgba(251,191,36,.3); }}
  #result {{ margin-top: 12px; }}
</style></head>
<body>
  <div class="meta">patch_hash: {patch_hash[:16]} \u00b7 exit_code: {exit_code}</div>
  <div>
    <button class="btn btn-run" onclick="runCode()">\u25b6 Run</button>
    <button class="btn btn-debug" onclick="debugCode()">\u2691 Debug &amp; Fix</button>
  </div>
  <div id="result"></div>
  <h2>Code</h2>
  <pre class="code">{code_html}</pre>
  {f'<h2>Output</h2><pre class="stdout">{stdout_html}</pre>' if stdout else ''}
  {f'<h2>Errors</h2><pre class="stderr">{stderr_html}</pre>' if stderr else ''}
<script>
async function runCode() {{
  const r = document.getElementById('result');
  r.innerHTML = '<h2>Running...</h2>';
  try {{
    const resp = await fetch('/run?patch_hash={patch_hash}', {{method:'POST'}});
    const data = await resp.json();
    let html = '';
    if (data.stdout) html += '<h2>Output</h2><pre class="stdout">' + data.stdout.replace(/</g,'&lt;') + '</pre>';
    if (data.stderr) html += '<h2>Errors</h2><pre class="stderr">' + data.stderr.replace(/</g,'&lt;') + '</pre>';
    html += '<div class="meta">exit_code: ' + data.exit_code + '</div>';
    if (data.exit_code !== 0) html += '<button class="btn btn-debug" onclick="debugCode()">\u2691 Auto-Fix with LLM</button>';
    r.innerHTML = html;
  }} catch(e) {{ r.innerHTML = '<pre class="stderr">' + e.message + '</pre>'; }}
}}
async function debugCode() {{
  const r = document.getElementById('result');
  r.innerHTML = '<h2>Debugging with LLM...</h2>';
  try {{
    const resp = await fetch('/debug?patch_hash={patch_hash}', {{method:'POST'}});
    const data = await resp.json();
    if (data.error) {{ r.innerHTML = '<pre class="stderr">' + data.error + '</pre>'; return; }}
    r.innerHTML = '<h2>Fixed! New hash: ' + data.patch_hash.slice(0,12) + '</h2><a class="btn btn-run" href="/artifact/' + data.patch_hash + '">View Fixed Artifact</a><button class="btn btn-run" onclick="location.href=\'/artifact/' + data.patch_hash + '\'">Open</button>';
  }} catch(e) {{ r.innerHTML = '<pre class="stderr">' + e.message + '</pre>'; }}
}}
</script>
</body></html>"""


@app.post("/store-artifact")
async def store_artifact(request: Request):
    """Store generated code + metadata for later viewing/running.
    Accepts JSON body or query params."""
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    patch_hash = body.get("patch_hash", "") or request.query_params.get("patch_hash", "")
    code = body.get("code", "") or request.query_params.get("code", "")
    reasoning = body.get("reasoning", "") or request.query_params.get("reasoning", "")
    evidence = body.get("evidence", "") or request.query_params.get("evidence", "")
    observer_output = body.get("observer_output", "") or request.query_params.get("observer_output", "")
    if not patch_hash:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "patch_hash required"}, 400)
    # If code doesn't look like Python, try extracting from observer_output
    if code and not code.strip().startswith(('import ', 'from ', 'def ', 'class ', 'print(', 'if ', 'for ', 'while ', '#', '"""', "'''", 'import\t', 'from\t')):
        code = extract_code_from_output(observer_output) or code
    if not code.strip():
        code = extract_code_from_output(observer_output)
    data = {"code": code, "reasoning": reasoning, "evidence": evidence, "observer_output": observer_output, "timestamp": time.time(), "patch_hash": patch_hash}
    artifact_store[patch_hash] = data
    save_artifact_disk(patch_hash, data)
    return {"stored": True, "patch_hash": patch_hash}


@app.get("/api/artifacts")
async def list_artifacts(limit: int = 50):
    """List all artifacts for the lab diary."""
    return {"artifacts": list_artifacts_disk(limit=limit)}


@app.get("/api/artifacts/{patch_hash}")
async def get_artifact_api(patch_hash: str):
    """Get a single artifact by hash."""
    data = artifact_store.get(patch_hash) or load_artifact_disk(patch_hash)
    if not data:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "not found"}, 404)
    return data


@app.post("/audio/upload")
async def upload_audio(request: Request, patch_hash: str = ""):
    """Receive raw binary audio recording and save to disk."""
    content = await request.body()
    if not content:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "No audio data received"}, 400)
    AUDIO_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    if patch_hash:
        filename = f"{patch_hash}.webm"
    else:
        filename = f"rec_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}.webm"
    audio_path = AUDIO_DIR / filename
    audio_path.write_bytes(content)
    audio_hash = hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest()
    if patch_hash:
        data = load_artifact_disk(patch_hash)
        if data:
            data["audio_path"] = filename
            data["audio_hash"] = audio_hash
            save_artifact_disk(patch_hash, data)
    return {
        "stored": True,
        "audio_file": filename,
        "size_bytes": len(content),
        "audio_hash": audio_hash,
        "linked_to": patch_hash if patch_hash else None,
    }


@app.post("/audio/store")
async def store_audio(request: Request):
    """Store base64-encoded audio recording alongside an artifact."""
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    patch_hash = body.get("patch_hash", "") or request.query_params.get("patch_hash", "")
    audio_b64 = body.get("audio_b64", "") or request.query_params.get("audio_b64", "")
    if not audio_b64:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "audio_b64 required"}, 400)
    AUDIO_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    if patch_hash:
        filename = f"{patch_hash}.webm"
    else:
        filename = f"rec_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}.webm"
    audio_path = AUDIO_DIR / filename
    audio_bytes = base64.b64decode(audio_b64)
    audio_path.write_bytes(audio_bytes)
    audio_hash = hashlib.sha256(audio_bytes).hexdigest()
    if patch_hash:
        data = load_artifact_disk(patch_hash)
        if data:
            data["audio_path"] = filename
            data["audio_hash"] = audio_hash
            save_artifact_disk(patch_hash, data)
    return {
        "stored": True,
        "audio_file": filename,
        "size_bytes": len(audio_bytes),
        "audio_hash": audio_hash,
        "linked_to": patch_hash if patch_hash else None,
    }


@app.get("/audio/list")
async def list_audio():
    """List all recorded audio files."""
    AUDIO_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    files = []
    for f in sorted(AUDIO_DIR.glob("*.webm"), key=lambda x: x.stat().st_mtime, reverse=True):
        files.append({
            "filename": f.name,
            "size_bytes": f.stat().st_size,
            "modified": f.stat().st_mtime,
        })
    return {"total": len(files), "files": files}


@app.get("/audio/{filename}")
async def get_audio(filename: str):
    """Serve an audio file."""
    path = AUDIO_DIR / filename
    if not path.exists():
        return JSONResponse({"error": "not found"}, 404)
    return FileResponse(str(path), media_type="audio/webm")


@app.websocket("/ws/stream")
async def ws_stream(ws: WebSocket):
    """Main WebSocket — receives frames + transcripts, runs the five loops."""
    await ws.accept()
    state = SessionState()
    sessions[state.session_id] = state
    logger_info = f"[WS] Camera/audio stream connected: session={state.session_id}"
    print(logger_info, flush=True)

    await ws.send_json({"type": "session", "session_id": state.session_id})

    try:
        while True:
            msg = await ws.receive_json()
            msg_type = msg.get("type")

            if msg_type == "frame":
                # ─── Loop 1: Capture ───
                print(f"[WS] Frame received: session={state.session_id} frames={len(state.frames)+1}", flush=True)
                previous_phash = state.frames[-1].phash if state.frames else None
                frame = process_frame(msg["frame"], previous_phash)
                state.add_frame(frame)

                # Adaptive FPS
                motion_scores = [f.motion_score for f in state.frames]
                if should_increase_fps(motion_scores):
                    state.frame_rate_sampled = min(4.0, state.frame_rate_sampled + 0.5)
                else:
                    state.frame_rate_sampled = max(1.0, state.frame_rate_sampled - 0.2)

                await ws.send_json({
                    "type": "frame_ack",
                    "sha256": frame.sha256,
                    "entropy": round(frame.entropy, 4),
                    "motion": round(frame.motion_score, 4),
                    "fps_adaptive": round(state.frame_rate_sampled, 1),
                    "buffered": len(state.frames),
                })

                # Update mode from frame message (no user instruction — intent is inferred)
                state.mode = msg.get("mode", state.mode)

                # ─── Loop 3: Novelty ───
                novelty = compute_novelty(state)
                state.novelty_score = novelty

                # ─── Loop 4: Compress + Observe ───
                if should_observe(state, novelty):
                    compact = compress_state(state)
                    try:
                        observation = observe(state, compact)
                        state.observer_state = observation
                        state.last_observer_ts = time.time()
                        state.scene_summary = observation.get("observer_output", "")[:200]

                        await ws.send_json({
                            "type": "observer",
                            "output": observation["observer_output"],
                            "state_hash": observation.get("state_hash", ""),
                            "novelty": round(novelty, 4),
                        })
                    except Exception as e:
                        await ws.send_json({"type": "error", "stage": "observer", "message": str(e)})

                # ─── Loop 5: Code generation (QVD gate) ───
                qvd = compute_qvd(state, novelty)
                state.qvd = qvd

                force = bool(msg.get("force", False))
                if force or (qvd > state.qvd_threshold and novelty > 0.1):
                    # ─── Evidence Gate: check if sufficient intent exists ───
                    ev_result = evidence_gate(
                        (state.observer_state or {}).get("observer_output", ""),
                        compress_state(state),
                    )
                    log_gate_decision("evidence_gate", ev_result, patch_hash="")
                    if not ev_result.passed:
                        print(f"[WS] Evidence Gate blocked: session={state.session_id} reason={ev_result.reason[:60]}", flush=True)
                        await ws.send_json({
                            "type": "gate_block",
                            "gate": "evidence_gate",
                            "reason": ev_result.reason,
                            "evidence_type": ev_result.evidence_type,
                            "qvd": round(qvd, 4),
                        })
                    else:
                        try:
                            result = build(state, state.observer_state or {})
                            state.current_artifact = result["patch_output"]
                            state.last_patch_hash = result["patch_hash"]
                            state.last_builder_ts = time.time()
                            state.receipt_count += 1

                            save_receipt(result["receipt"])
                            print(f"[WS] Patch generated: session={state.session_id} hash={result['patch_hash'][:16]} qvd={round(qvd,3)}", flush=True)

                            await ws.send_json({
                                "type": "patch",
                                "output": result["patch_output"],
                                "patch_hash": result["patch_hash"],
                                "receipt": result["receipt"],
                                "qvd": round(qvd, 4),
                            })
                        except Exception as e:
                            await ws.send_json({"type": "error", "stage": "builder", "message": str(e)})

                # Send QVD update
                await ws.send_json({
                    "type": "qvd",
                    "value": round(qvd, 4),
                    "threshold": state.qvd_threshold,
                    "novelty": round(novelty, 4),
                })

            elif msg_type == "transcript":
                # ─── Loop 2: Audio ───
                text = (msg.get("text") or "").strip()
                if text:
                    print(f"[WS] Transcript received: session={state.session_id} speaker=auto text={text[:60]}", flush=True)
                    speaker = assign_speaker(text, state.speakers)
                    chunk = AudioChunk(
                        ts=time.time(),
                        chunk_id=f"chunk_{int(time.time() * 1000)}",
                        duration_ms=len(text) * 50,
                        speaker=speaker,
                        transcript=text,
                        confidence=0.85,
                        sha256=hashlib.sha256(text.encode()).hexdigest(),
                        is_speech=True,
                    )
                    state.add_audio_chunk(chunk)

                    await ws.send_json({
                        "type": "transcript_ack",
                        "speaker": speaker,
                        "chars": len(text),
                        "chunk_id": chunk.chunk_id,
                    })

            elif msg_type == "synthesize":
                # Force full cycle
                compact = compress_state(state)
                observation = observe(state, compact)
                state.observer_state = observation
                state.last_observer_ts = time.time()

                # ─── Evidence Gate: check if sufficient intent exists ───
                ev_result = evidence_gate(observation.get("observer_output", ""), compact)
                log_gate_decision("evidence_gate", ev_result, patch_hash="")
                if not ev_result.passed:
                    print(f"[WS] Evidence Gate blocked (synthesize): session={state.session_id} reason={ev_result.reason[:60]}", flush=True)
                    await ws.send_json({
                        "type": "gate_block",
                        "gate": "evidence_gate",
                        "reason": ev_result.reason,
                        "evidence_type": ev_result.evidence_type,
                    })
                else:
                    result = build(state, observation)
                    state.current_artifact = result["patch_output"]
                    state.last_patch_hash = result["patch_hash"]
                    state.last_builder_ts = time.time()
                    state.receipt_count += 1
                    save_receipt(result["receipt"])
                    print(f"[WS] Patch generated (synthesize): session={state.session_id} hash={result['patch_hash'][:16]}", flush=True)

                    await ws.send_json({
                        "type": "patch",
                        "output": result["patch_output"],
                        "patch_hash": result["patch_hash"],
                        "receipt": result["receipt"],
                    })

            elif msg_type == "state":
                await ws.send_json({
                    "type": "state",
                    "state": state.to_dict(),
                })

            elif msg_type == "ping":
                await ws.send_json({"type": "pong", "session_id": state.session_id})

            else:
                await ws.send_json({"type": "error", "message": f"Unknown type: {msg_type}"})

    except WebSocketDisconnect:
        print(f"[WS] Stream disconnected: session={state.session_id} frames={len(state.frames)} receipts={state.receipt_count}", flush=True)
        sessions.pop(state.session_id, None)


# ─── Long-Running Terminal Sessions ───


@app.get("/ws/health")
async def ws_health():
    """Report WebSocket stream status — proves camera/audio ingestion capability."""
    active_ws_sessions = [s for s in sessions.values() if s.frames or s.audio_chunks]
    return {
        "websocket_endpoint": "/ws/stream",
        "protocol": "WebSocket",
        "active_stream_sessions": len(active_ws_sessions),
        "total_sessions": len(sessions),
        "sessions_detail": [
            {
                "session_id": s.session_id,
                "frames_captured": len(s.frames),
                "audio_chunks": len(s.audio_chunks),
                "has_observer_state": bool(s.observer_state),
                "patches_generated": s.receipt_count,
                "last_patch_hash": s.last_patch_hash[:16] if s.last_patch_hash else None,
                "mode": s.mode,
                "qvd": round(s.qvd, 4) if s.qvd else 0,
                "qvd_threshold": s.qvd_threshold,
            }
            for s in active_ws_sessions
        ],
        "supported_message_types": ["frame", "transcript", "synthesize", "state", "ping"],
        "camera_support": {
            "iphone_continuity": True,
            "webcam": True,
            "video_file_upload": True,
            "adaptive_fps": "1-4 FPS based on motion/novelty",
        },
        "audio_support": {
            "microphone": True,
            "transcription": True,
            "speaker_diarization": True,
        },
    }


class TerminalSession:
    """Persistent terminal session for long-running processes like backtests."""
    def __init__(self, session_id: str, cwd: str = "/tmp", shell: str = "/bin/bash"):
        self.session_id = session_id
        self.cwd = cwd
        self.shell = shell
        self.proc = None
        self.output_buffer = deque(maxlen=10000)
        self.created_at = time.time()
        self.last_command = None
        self.lock = threading.Lock()
        self._reader_thread = None

    def start(self):
        self.proc = subprocess.Popen(
            [self.shell, "--norc", "--noprofile"],
            stdin=subprocess.PIPE,
            stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
            stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
            text=True,
            cwd=self.cwd,
            bufsize=1,
            env={**os.environ, "PS1": "csc$ ", "TERM": "dumb", "BASH_ENV": ""},
            start_new_session=True,
        )
        self._reader_thread = threading.Thread(target=self._read_output, daemon=True)
        self._reader_thread.start()

    def _read_output(self):
        try:
            for line in self.proc.stdout:
                self.output_buffer.append({"ts": time.time(), "line": line})
        except Exception:
            pass

    def exec(self, command: str, timeout: float = 300) -> dict:
        with self.lock:
            if not self.proc or self.proc.poll() is not None:
                return {"error": "Session terminated", "exit_code": self.proc.returncode if self.proc else -1}
            self.last_command = command
            buf_start = len(self.output_buffer)
            try:
                self.proc.stdin.write(command + "\n")
                self.proc.stdin.flush()
            except (BrokenPipeError, OSError):
                return {"error": "Session pipe broken", "exit_code": -1}
            # Wait for output to stabilize (no new lines for 500ms) or timeout
            time.sleep(0.3)
            deadline = time.time() + timeout
            last_count = 0
            while time.time() < deadline:
                current = len(self.output_buffer)
                if current == last_count and current > buf_start:
                    break
                last_count = current
                time.sleep(0.5)
            new_lines = list(self.output_buffer)[buf_start:]
            output = "".join(item["line"] for item in new_lines)
            return {"output": output[-8000:], "lines": len(new_lines), "command": command}

    def get_output(self, since: int = 0) -> dict:
        lines = [item for item in self.output_buffer if item["ts"] >= since]
        return {
            "output": "".join(item["line"] for item in lines)[-16000:],
            "line_count": len(self.output_buffer),
            "is_alive": self.proc and self.proc.poll() is None,
        }

    def kill(self):
        if self.proc and self.proc.poll() is None:
            try:
                os.killpg(os.getpgid(self.proc.pid), signal.SIGTERM)
            except (ProcessLookupError, PermissionError):
                self.proc.kill()
        return {"killed": True, "session_id": self.session_id}

    def status(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "session_id": self.session_id,
            "alive": self.proc and self.proc.poll() is None,
            "created_at": self.created_at,
            "uptime_s": round(time.time() - self.created_at, 1),
            "last_command": self.last_command,
            "buffer_lines": len(self.output_buffer),
            "cwd": self.cwd,
        }


terminal_sessions: dict[str, TerminalSession] = {}


@app.post("/terminal/create")
async def terminal_create(request: Request):
    """Create a long-running terminal session for backtesting, training, etc."""
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    cwd = body.get("cwd", "/tmp") or request.query_params.get("cwd", "/tmp")
    sid = f"term_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}"
    session = TerminalSession(sid, cwd=cwd)
    session.start()
    terminal_sessions[sid] = session
    return {"session_id": sid, "created_at": session.created_at, "cwd": cwd}


@app.post("/terminal/{session_id}/exec")
async def terminal_exec(session_id: str, request: Request):
    """Execute a command in a terminal session. Supports long-running commands."""
    # Hardening: rate limit + auth + command policy
    ip = _client_ip(request)
    if _rate_limited(ip):
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Rate limit exceeded", "retry_after": RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW}, 429)
    if not _check_exec_auth(request):
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Unauthorized — auth token required"}, 401)

    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    command = body.get("command", "") or request.query_params.get("command", "")
    timeout = float(body.get("timeout", 300) or request.query_params.get("timeout", 300))

    # Command policy: block dangerous commands
    if _is_command_blocked(command):
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Command blocked by safety policy", "command": command[:100]}, 403)

    if session_id not in terminal_sessions:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Terminal session not found"}, 404)
    session = terminal_sessions[session_id]
    if not command.strip():
        return JSONResponse({"error": "No command provided"}, 400)
    return session.exec(command, timeout=timeout)


@app.get("/terminal/{session_id}/output")
async def terminal_output(session_id: str, since: float = 0):
    """Get accumulated output from a terminal session."""
    if session_id not in terminal_sessions:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Terminal session not found"}, 404)
    return terminal_sessions[session_id].get_output(since=since)


@app.post("/terminal/{session_id}/kill")
async def terminal_kill(session_id: str):
    """Kill a terminal session."""
    if session_id not in terminal_sessions:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Terminal session not found"}, 404)
    result = terminal_sessions[session_id].kill()
    del terminal_sessions[session_id]
    return result


@app.get("/terminal/{session_id}/status")
async def terminal_status(session_id: str):
    """Get status of a terminal session."""
    if session_id not in terminal_sessions:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Terminal session not found"}, 404)
    return terminal_sessions[session_id].status()


@app.get("/terminal/sessions")
async def terminal_list():
    """List all active terminal sessions."""
    return {"sessions": [s.status() for s in terminal_sessions.values()]}


# ─── Gate Status ───

@app.get("/gate/status")
async def gate_status():
    """Show Builder Gate and Evidence Gate decisions and rejection history."""
    log = get_gate_log()
    builder_decisions = [e for e in log if e["gate_type"] == "builder_gate"]
    evidence_decisions = [e for e in log if e["gate_type"] == "evidence_gate"]
    return {
        "total_decisions": len(log),
        "builder_gate": {
            "total": len(builder_decisions),
            "passed": sum(1 for e in builder_decisions if e["passed"]),
            "rejected": sum(1 for e in builder_decisions if not e["passed"]),
            "recent": builder_decisions[-10:],
        },
        "evidence_gate": {
            "total": len(evidence_decisions),
            "passed": sum(1 for e in evidence_decisions if e["passed"]),
            "rejected": sum(1 for e in evidence_decisions if not e["passed"]),
            "recent": evidence_decisions[-10:],
        },
    }


# ─── Universal Hash Resolver ───

@app.get("/resolve/{hash_value}")
async def resolve_hash(hash_value: str):
    """Universal hash resolver — any hash from patch generation, debugging, 
    receipt creation, or artifact storage resolves to a typed object.
    
    Returns: {type: artifact|receipt|patch|missing, data: {...}}
    """
    # 1. Check in-memory artifact store
    if hash_value in artifact_store:
        return {"type": "artifact", "source": "memory", "hash": hash_value, "data": artifact_store[hash_value]}
    
    # 2. Check artifact disk
    artifact = load_artifact_disk(hash_value)
    if artifact and artifact.get("patch_hash"):
        return {"type": "artifact", "source": "disk", "hash": hash_value, "data": artifact}
    
    # 3. Check receipt disk
    receipt = get_receipt(hash_value)
    if receipt:
        return {"type": "receipt", "source": "disk", "hash": hash_value, "data": receipt}
    
    # 4. Check if it's a debug artifact (debug patches have _debug suffix in hash generation)
    debug_artifact = load_artifact_disk(hash_value)
    if debug_artifact and debug_artifact.get("debug"):
        return {"type": "debug_artifact", "source": "disk", "hash": hash_value, "data": debug_artifact}
    
    # 5. Not found — return typed missing response
    return JSONResponse({
        "type": "missing",
        "hash": hash_value,
        "checked": ["artifact_store", "artifact_disk", "receipt_disk"],
        "suggestion": "Hash may be from a previous session that was not persisted, or from an in-memory store that was cleared on restart.",
    }, 404)


# ─── Endpoint-Compatible API (for ChatGPT GPT Actions) ───
# These endpoints mirror josephrw/Endpoint so ChatGPT can use csc-engine as its backend

@app.get("/capabilities")
async def capabilities():
    """List all capabilities — ChatGPT discovery endpoint."""
    return {
        "app": "csc-engine",
        "version": "1.0.0",
        "capabilities": [
            "terminal.run", "python.run", "files.write", "files.read", "files.list",
            "workspace.tree", "artifact.compile", "web.scrape", "sessions",
            "receipts", "gate.status", "resolve.hash", "camera.stream", "audio.record",
        ],
        "limits": {
            "max_timeout_seconds": 30,
            "max_output_bytes": 120000,
            "rate_limit": "30/min per IP",
        },
        "gates": {"builder_gate": True, "evidence_gate": True},
    }


@app.post("/terminal/run")
async def terminal_run(request: Request):
    """Execute a shell command (Endpoint-compatible). Subject to Builder Gate for Python, command blocking for shell."""
    ip = _client_ip(request)
    if _rate_limited(ip):
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Rate limit exceeded"}, 429)
    if not _check_exec_auth(request):
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Unauthorized"}, 401)

    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    command = body.get("command", "")
    timeout = min(float(body.get("timeout", 30)), 30)
    workspace = body.get("workspace", "default")

    if _is_command_blocked(command):
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Command blocked by safety policy", "command": command[:100]}, 403)

    try:
        result = subprocess.run(
            command, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True,
            timeout=timeout, cwd="/tmp"
        )
        output = (result.stdout + result.stderr)[-120000:]
        return {
            "output": output,
            "exit_code": result.returncode,
            "command": command[:200],
        }
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Command timed out", "timeout": timeout}, 408)
    except Exception as e:
        return JSONResponse({"error": str(e)}, 500)


@app.post("/python/run")
async def python_run(request: Request):
    """Execute Python code (Endpoint-compatible). Goes through Builder Gate."""
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    code = body.get("code", "")
    timeout = min(float(body.get("timeout", 30)), 30)

    gate_result = builder_gate(code)
    log_gate_decision("builder_gate", gate_result, patch_hash="")
    if not gate_result.passed:
        return JSONResponse({
            "error": "Code rejected by Builder Gate",
            "gate_reason": gate_result.reason,
            "gate_checks": gate_result.checks,
        }, 422)

    try:
        with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(mode="w", suffix=".py", delete=False) as f:
            f.write(code)
            f.flush()
            result = subprocess.run(
                ["python3", f.name],
                capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=timeout
            )
        output = (result.stdout + result.stderr)[-120000:]
        return {
            "stdout": result.stdout,
            "stderr": result.stderr,
            "output": output,
            "exit_code": result.returncode,
        }
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Execution timed out", "timeout": timeout}, 408)
    except Exception as e:
        return JSONResponse({"error": str(e)}, 500)


@app.post("/files/write")
async def files_write(request: Request):
    """Write a file to the workspace."""
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    workspace = body.get("workspace", "default")
    path = body.get("path", "")
    content = body.get("content", "")

    if not path or ".." in path:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Invalid path"}, 400)

    ws_dir = DATA_DIR / "workspaces" / workspace
    ws_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    file_path = ws_dir / path
    file_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    file_path.write_text(content)
    return {"status": "written", "path": path, "bytes": len(content)}

@app.post("/files/list")
async def files_list(request: Request):
    """List files in a workspace."""
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    workspace = body.get("workspace", "default")
    ws_dir = DATA_DIR / "workspaces" / workspace
    if not ws_dir.exists():
        return {"files": [], "workspace": workspace}
    files = []
    for f in ws_dir.rglob("*"):
        if f.is_file():
            files.append({"path": str(f.relative_to(ws_dir)), "size": f.stat().st_size})
    return {"files": files[:100], "workspace": workspace}

        return JSONResponse({"error": "Invalid path"}, 400)

    file_path = DATA_DIR / "workspaces" / workspace / path
    if not file_path.exists():
        return JSONResponse({"error": "File not found", "path": path}, 404)
    file_path.unlink()
    return {"status": "deleted", "path": path}


@app.get("/workspace/tree")
async def workspace_tree(workspace: str = "default"):
    """Get workspace file tree as text."""
    ws_dir = DATA_DIR / "workspaces" / workspace
    if not ws_dir.exists():
        return f"(empty workspace: {workspace})"
    lines = []
    for f in sorted(ws_dir.rglob("*")):
        rel = f.relative_to(ws_dir)
        prefix = "  " * (str(rel).count("/") )
        if f.is_file():
            lines.append(f"{prefix}{f.name} ({f.stat().st_size}B)")
        else:
            lines.append(f"{prefix}{f.name}/")
    return "\n".join(lines[:200])


@app.post("/web/scrape")
async def web_scrape(request: Request):
    """Scrape a public URL and return text content."""
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    url = body.get("url", "")
    if not url or not url.startswith(("http://", "https://")):
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Invalid URL"}, 400)

    import urllib.request
    try:
        req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": "CSC-Engine/1.0"})
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=15) as resp:
            content = resp.read(1500000).decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
            return {"url": url, "content": content[:200000], "status": resp.status}
    except Exception as e:
        return JSONResponse({"error": str(e)}, 500)

        pass
    session_id = f"gpt_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}"
    return {"session_id": session_id, "created_at": time.time()}

# ─── Kernel IDE Endpoints (Devin-like) ───

@app.post("/fs/mkdir")
async def fs_mkdir(request: Request):
    """Create a directory in the workspace."""
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    workspace = body.get("workspace", "default")
    path = body.get("path", "")
    if not path or ".." in path:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Invalid path"}, 400)
    dir_path = DATA_DIR / "workspaces" / workspace / path
    dir_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    return {"status": "created", "path": path}


@app.post("/fs/move")
async def fs_move(request: Request):
    """Move/rename a file or directory."""
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    workspace = body.get("workspace", "default")
    src = body.get("src", "")
    dst = body.get("dst", "")
    if not src or not dst or ".." in src or ".." in dst:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Invalid paths"}, 400)
    ws = DATA_DIR / "workspaces" / workspace
    src_path = ws / src
    dst_path = ws / dst
    if not src_path.exists():
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Source not found", "src": src}, 404)
    dst_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    src_path.rename(dst_path)
    return {"status": "moved", "src": src, "dst": dst}


@app.post("/fs/copy")
async def fs_copy(request: Request):
    """Copy a file or directory."""
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    workspace = body.get("workspace", "default")
    src = body.get("src", "")
    dst = body.get("dst", "")
    if not src or not dst or ".." in src or ".." in dst:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Invalid paths"}, 400)
    ws = DATA_DIR / "workspaces" / workspace
    src_path = ws / src
    dst_path = ws / dst
    if not src_path.exists():
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Source not found", "src": src}, 404)
    import shutil
    dst_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    if src_path.is_dir():
        shutil.copytree(src_path, dst_path)
    else:
        shutil.copy2(src_path, dst_path)
    return {"status": "copied", "src": src, "dst": dst}


@app.post("/fs/chmod")
async def fs_chmod(request: Request):
    """Change file permissions."""
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    workspace = body.get("workspace", "default")
    path = body.get("path", "")
    mode = body.get("mode", "644")
    if not path or ".." in path:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Invalid path"}, 400)
    file_path = DATA_DIR / "workspaces" / workspace / path
    if not file_path.exists():
        return JSONResponse({"error": "File not found", "path": path}, 404)
    try:
        mode_int = int(str(mode), 8)
        file_path.chmod(mode_int)
        return {"status": "chmod", "path": path, "mode": mode}
    except (ValueError, OSError) as e:
        return JSONResponse({"error": str(e)}, 400)


@app.post("/fs/search")
async def fs_search(request: Request):
    """Search file contents (grep-like)."""
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    workspace = body.get("workspace", "default")
    pattern = body.get("pattern", "")
    file_pattern = body.get("file_pattern", "*")
    if not pattern:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "No pattern provided"}, 400)
    ws_dir = DATA_DIR / "workspaces" / workspace
    if not ws_dir.exists():
        return {"matches": [], "workspace": workspace}
    import re
    try:
        regex = re.compile(pattern, re.IGNORECASE)
    except re.error:
        regex = re.compile(re.escape(pattern))
    matches = []
    for f in ws_dir.rglob(file_pattern):
        if not f.is_file() or f.stat().st_size > 500000:
            continue
        try:
            for i, line in enumerate(f.read_text(errors="replace").splitlines(), 1):
                if regex.search(line):
                    matches.append({"file": str(f.relative_to(ws_dir)), "line": i, "text": line[:200]})
                    if len(matches) >= 50:
                        return {"matches": matches, "truncated": True}
        except Exception:
            pass
    return {"matches": matches, "truncated": False}

async def git_init(request: Request):
    """Initialize a git repo in a workspace."""

    result = subprocess.run(
        "git init && git config user.email 'csc-engine@hf.space' && git config user.name 'CSC Engine'",
        shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, cwd=str(ws_dir)
    )
    return {"output": result.stdout + result.stderr, "exit_code": result.returncode}


@app.post("/git/status")
async def git_status(request: Request):
    """Get git status for a workspace."""
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    workspace = body.get("workspace", "default")
    ws_dir = DATA_DIR / "workspaces" / workspace

    )
    files = []
    for line in result.stdout.strip().splitlines():

    return {"files": files, "clean": len(files) == 0}

@app.post("/git/log")
async def git_log(request: Request):
    """Get git log."""
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    workspace = body.get("workspace", "default")
    limit = body.get("limit", 20)
    ws_dir = DATA_DIR / "workspaces" / workspace
    result = subprocess.run(
        f"git log --oneline --all -{limit}",
        shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, cwd=str(ws_dir)
    )
    commits = []
    for line in result.stdout.strip().splitlines():
        parts = line.split(" ", 1)
        if len(parts) == 2:
            commits.append({"hash": parts[0], "message": parts[1]})
    return {"commits": commits}
