@app.post("/git/branch")
async def git_branch(request: Request):
    """Create or list branches."""
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    workspace = body.get("workspace", "default")
    name = body.get("name", "")
    ws_dir = DATA_DIR / "workspaces" / workspace
    if name:
        result = subprocess.run(
            f"git checkout -b {name}",
            shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, cwd=str(ws_dir)
        )
        return {"output": result.stdout + result.stderr, "exit_code": result.returncode}
    else:
        result = subprocess.run(
            "git branch -a", shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, cwd=str(ws_dir)
        )
        branches = [b.strip().lstrip("* ") for b in result.stdout.strip().splitlines()]
        return {"branches": branches}


@app.post("/pkg/install")
async def pkg_install(request: Request):
    """Install a pip package."""
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    package = body.get("package", "")
    if not package:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "No package specified"}, 400)
    # Block dangerous packages
    dangerous = [";", "&&", "|", "`", "$(", "rm ", "sudo"]
    if any(d in package for d in dangerous):
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Invalid package name"}, 400)
    result = subprocess.run(
        f"pip install {package}",
        shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=120
    )
    return {
        "output": (result.stdout + result.stderr)[-50000:],
        "exit_code": result.returncode,
        "package": package,
    }


@app.get("/pkg/list")
async def pkg_list():
    """List installed pip packages."""
    result = subprocess.run(
        "pip list --format=columns", shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True
    )
    return {"packages": result.stdout}


@app.post("/proc/list")
async def proc_list(request: Request):
    """List running processes."""
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    pattern = body.get("pattern", "")
    cmd = "ps aux"
    if pattern:
        cmd += f" | grep '{pattern.replace("'", "'\\''")}' | grep -v grep"
    result = subprocess.run(cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True)
    processes = []
    for line in result.stdout.strip().splitlines()[1:]:
        parts = line.split(None, 10)
        if len(parts) >= 11:
            processes.append({
                "user": parts[0], "pid": int(parts[1]), "cpu": parts[2],
                "mem": parts[3], "command": parts[10][:200],
            })
    return {"processes": processes[:100]}


@app.post("/proc/kill")
async def proc_kill(request: Request):
    """Kill a process by PID."""
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    pid = body.get("pid", 0)
    if not pid or pid == 1:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Cannot kill PID 1 or invalid PID"}, 400)
    try:
        os.kill(int(pid), 15)
        return {"status": "killed", "pid": pid}
    except ProcessLookupError:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Process not found", "pid": pid}, 404)
    except PermissionError:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Permission denied", "pid": pid}, 403)


@app.get("/sys/info")
async def sys_info():
    """Get system information — OS, CPU, memory, disk."""
    import platform
    uname = platform.uname()
    # Get memory info
    mem = {}
    try:
        with open("/proc/meminfo") as f:
            for line in f:
                k, v = line.split(":", 1)
                mem[k.strip()] = v.strip()
    except Exception:
        pass
    # Get disk usage
    disk = {}
    try:
        result = subprocess.run("df -h /", shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True)
        disk["root"] = result.stdout
    except Exception:
        pass
    # Get CPU info
    cpu_count = os.cpu_count()
    return {
        "os": uname.system,
        "release": uname.release,
        "machine": uname.machine,
        "processor": uname.processor,
        "cpu_count": cpu_count,
        "memory": mem,
        "disk": disk["root"] if disk else "",
        "python_version": platform.python_version(),
        "provider": os.getenv("PROVIDER", "unknown"),
    }


@app.get("/env")
async def get_env():
    """Get non-secret environment variables."""
    safe_keys = ["PROVIDER", "PORT", "HF_TEXT_MODEL", "GROQ_TEXT_MODEL",
                 "OLLAMA_MODEL", "OPENAI_MODEL", "PATH"]
    env = {}
    for key in safe_keys:
        val = os.getenv(key)
        if val:
            env[key] = val
    return {"env": env}


@app.post("/exec")
async def exec_command(request: Request):
    """Execute a command with working directory in workspace.
    This is the main IDE terminal endpoint — supports cwd, env vars, timeout."""
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    command = body.get("command", "")
    workspace = body.get("workspace", "default")
    timeout = min(float(body.get("timeout", 30)), 60)

    if not command:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "No command provided"}, 400)
    if _is_command_blocked(command):
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Command blocked by safety policy"}, 403)

    ws_dir = DATA_DIR / "workspaces" / workspace
    ws_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    try:
        result = subprocess.run(
            command, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True,
            timeout=timeout, cwd=str(ws_dir)
        )
        return {
            "stdout": result.stdout,
            "stderr": result.stderr,
            "output": (result.stdout + result.stderr)[-120000:],
            "exit_code": result.returncode,
            "command": command,
            "cwd": str(ws_dir),
        }
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Command timed out", "timeout": timeout}, 408)
    except Exception as e:
        return JSONResponse({"error": str(e)}, 500)


# ─── Production: Metrics & Monitoring ───

_metrics: dict = {"requests_total": 0, "errors_total": 0, "start_time": time.time(), "endpoints": {}}

def _track_metric(path: str, status: int):
    _metrics["requests_total"] += 1
    if status >= 400:
        _metrics["errors_total"] += 1
    key = path
    if key not in _metrics["endpoints"]:
        _metrics["endpoints"][key] = {"count": 0, "errors": 0}
    _metrics["endpoints"][key]["count"] += 1
    if status >= 400:
        _metrics["endpoints"][key]["errors"] += 1


@app.middleware("http")
async def metrics_middleware(request: Request, call_next):
    response = await call_next(request)
    _track_metric(request.url.path, response.status_code)
    return response


@app.get("/metrics")
async def metrics():
    uptime = time.time() - _metrics["start_time"]
    return {
        "requests_total": _metrics["requests_total"],
        "errors_total": _metrics["errors_total"],
        "uptime_seconds": round(uptime, 1),
        "error_rate": round(_metrics["errors_total"] / max(1, _metrics["requests_total"]), 4),
        "endpoints": _metrics["endpoints"],
        "active_sessions": len(sessions),
        "artifacts_stored": len(artifact_store),
        "rate_limit_max": RATE_LIMIT_MAX,
        "rate_limit_window": RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW,
    }


@app.get("/health/detailed")
async def health_detailed():
    checks = {}
    checks["api"] = {"status": "ok"}
    checks["sessions"] = {"count": len(sessions), "status": "ok"}
    checks["artifacts"] = {"count": len(artifact_store), "disk": str(ARTIFACT_DIR.exists())}
    checks["receipts"] = {"disk": str(RECEIPT_STORAGE_DIR.exists())}
    checks["workspaces"] = {"disk": str((DATA_DIR / "workspaces").exists())}
    try:
        import sqlite3
        conn = sqlite3.connect(":memory:")
        conn.execute("SELECT 1")
        conn.close()
        checks["sqlite"] = {"status": "ok"}
    except Exception as e:
        checks["sqlite"] = {"status": "error", "detail": str(e)}
    try:
        r = subprocess.run("python3 --version", shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5)
        checks["python"] = {"status": "ok", "version": r.stdout.strip()}
    except Exception:
        checks["python"] = {"status": "error"}
    try:
        r = subprocess.run("R --version 2>/dev/null | head -1", shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5)
        checks["r"] = {"status": "ok", "version": r.stdout.strip()}
    except Exception:
        checks["r"] = {"status": "error"}
    all_ok = all(v.get("status") == "ok" or v.get("disk") == "True" for v in checks.values())
    return {"status": "ok" if all_ok else "degraded", "checks": checks, "timestamp": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()}


# ─── Production: Universal Language Runner ───

LANG_MAP = {
    "python": ("python3", [".py"]),
    "python3": ("python3", [".py"]),
    "r": ("Rscript", [".r", ".R"]),
    "rscript": ("Rscript", [".r", ".R"]),
    "node": ("node", [".js"]),
    "javascript": ("node", [".js"]),
    "js": ("node", [".js"]),
    "ruby": ("ruby", [".rb"]),
    "rb": ("ruby", [".rb"]),
    "go": ("go run", [".go"]),
    "rust": ("rustc -o /tmp/_rust_bin && /tmp/_rust_bin", [".rs"]),
    "c": ("gcc -o /tmp/_c_bin && /tmp/_c_bin", [".c"]),
    "cpp": ("g++ -o /tmp/_cpp_bin && /tmp/_cpp_bin", [".cpp", ".cc", ".cxx"]),
    "c++": ("g++ -o /tmp/_cpp_bin && /tmp/_cpp_bin", [".cpp", ".cc", ".cxx"]),
    "java": ("javac && java", [".java"]),
    "php": ("php", [".php"]),
    "lua": ("lua5.4", [".lua"]),
    "perl": ("perl", [".pl", ".pm"]),
    "bash": ("bash", [".sh"]),
    "sh": ("sh", [".sh"]),
    "tcl": ("tclsh", [".tcl"]),
    "fortran": ("gfortran -o /tmp/_f_bin && /tmp/_f_bin", [".f", ".f90", ".f95"]),
    "haskell": ("runghc", [".hs"]),
    "ada": ("gnatmake -q && ./main", [".adb"]),
    "erlang": ("escript", [".erl"]),
    "elixir": ("elixir", [".ex", ".exs"]),
    "ocaml": ("ocaml", [".ml"]),
    "cobol": ("cobc -x -o /tmp/_cobol_bin && /tmp/_cobol_bin", [".cob", ".cbl"]),
    "julia": ("julia", [".jl"]),
    "dart": ("dart run", [".dart"]),
    "awk": ("awk -f", [".awk"]),
}


@app.post("/lang/execute")
async def lang_execute(request: Request):
    """Execute code in any supported language. Auto-detects or uses explicit language field."""
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    code = body.get("code", "")
    language = body.get("language", "").lower().strip()
    workspace = body.get("workspace", "default")
    timeout = min(float(body.get("timeout", 30)), 60)

    if not code:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "No code provided"}, 400)

    if language not in LANG_MAP:
        return JSONResponse({"error": f"Unsupported language: {language}", "supported": list(LANG_MAP.keys())}, 400)

    runner, exts = LANG_MAP[language]
    ws_dir = DATA_DIR / "workspaces" / workspace
    ws_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    ext = exts[0]
    filename = f"_exec_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}{ext}"
    filepath = ws_dir / filename
    filepath.write_text(code)

    # Build command based on language
    if language in ("c", "cpp", "c++", "rust", "fortran", "cobol"):
        if language == "c":
            cmd = f"gcc -o /tmp/_c_bin {filepath} && /tmp/_c_bin"
        elif language in ("cpp", "c++"):
            cmd = f"g++ -o /tmp/_cpp_bin {filepath} && /tmp/_cpp_bin"
        elif language == "rust":
            cmd = f"rustc -o /tmp/_rust_bin {filepath} && /tmp/_rust_bin"
        elif language == "fortran":
            cmd = f"gfortran -o /tmp/_f_bin {filepath} && /tmp/_f_bin"
        elif language == "cobol":
            cmd = f"cobc -x -o /tmp/_cobol_bin {filepath} && /tmp/_cobol_bin"
    elif language == "java":
        cmd = f"cd {ws_dir} && javac {filename} && java {filename.replace('.java', '')}"
    elif language == "ada":
        cmd = f"cd {ws_dir} && gnatmake -q {filename} && ./{filename.replace('.adb', '')}"
    elif language == "go":
        cmd = f"cd {ws_dir} && [ -f go.mod ] || go mod init exec_temp && go run {filename}"
    elif language == "awk":
        cmd = f"awk -f {filepath}"
    else:
        cmd = f"{runner} {filepath}"

    try:
        result = subprocess.run(cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=timeout, cwd=str(ws_dir))
        output = (result.stdout + result.stderr)[-120000:]
        exit_code = result.returncode
        try:
            filepath.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        except Exception:
            pass
        return {
            "language": language,
            "output": output,
            "stdout": result.stdout,
            "stderr": result.stderr,
            "exit_code": exit_code,
            "filename": filename,
        }
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Execution timed out", "timeout": timeout}, 408)
    except Exception as e:
        return JSONResponse({"error": str(e)}, 500)


@app.get("/lang/list")
async def lang_list():
    """List all supported languages."""
    verified = {}
    for lang, (runner, exts) in LANG_MAP.items():
        base_cmd = runner.split()[0]
        r = subprocess.run(f"which {base_cmd} 2>/dev/null", shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True)
        verified[lang] = {
            "runner": runner,
            "extensions": exts,
            "available": bool(r.stdout.strip()),
            "path": r.stdout.strip(),
        }
    return {"languages": verified, "total": len(verified)}


# ─── Production: Workspace Management ───

@app.post("/workspace/create")
async def workspace_create(request: Request):
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    name = body.get("name", "")
    if not name or ".." in name or "/" in name:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Invalid workspace name"}, 400)
    ws_dir = DATA_DIR / "workspaces" / name
    if ws_dir.exists():
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Workspace already exists", "name": name}, 409)
    ws_dir.mkdir(parents=True)
    return {"status": "created", "workspace": name, "path": str(ws_dir)}


@app.get("/workspace/list")
async def workspace_list():
    ws_root = DATA_DIR / "workspaces"
    if not ws_root.exists():
        return {"workspaces": []}
    workspaces = []
    for d in ws_root.iterdir():
        if d.is_dir():
            file_count = sum(1 for f in d.rglob("*") if f.is_file())
            size = sum(f.stat().st_size for f in d.rglob("*") if f.is_file())
            workspaces.append({"name": d.name, "files": file_count, "size_bytes": size})
    return {"workspaces": workspaces}


@app.post("/workspace/delete")
async def workspace_delete(request: Request):
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    name = body.get("name", "")
    if not name or ".." in name or "/" in name:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Invalid workspace name"}, 400)
    ws_dir = DATA_DIR / "workspaces" / name
    if not ws_dir.exists():
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Workspace not found", "name": name}, 404)
    import shutil
    shutil.rmtree(ws_dir)
    return {"status": "deleted", "workspace": name}


@app.post("/workspace/clone")
async def workspace_clone(request: Request):
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    src = body.get("src", "")
    dst = body.get("dst", "")
    if not src or not dst or ".." in src or ".." in dst:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Invalid workspace names"}, 400)
    ws_root = DATA_DIR / "workspaces"
    src_dir = ws_root / src
    dst_dir = ws_root / dst
    if not src_dir.exists():
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Source workspace not found"}, 404)
    if dst_dir.exists():
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Destination already exists"}, 409)
    import shutil
    shutil.copytree(src_dir, dst_dir)
    return {"status": "cloned", "src": src, "dst": dst}

@app.post("/git/push")
async def git_push(request: Request):
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    workspace = body.get("workspace", "default")
    remote = body.get("remote", "origin")
    branch = body.get("branch", "")
    ws_dir = DATA_DIR / "workspaces" / workspace
    cmd = f"git push {remote} {branch}".strip()
    result = subprocess.run(cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, cwd=str(ws_dir), timeout=30)
    return {"output": result.stdout + result.stderr, "exit_code": result.returncode}

    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    workspace = body.get("workspace", "default")
    remote = body.get("remote", "origin")
    branch = body.get("branch", "")
    ws_dir = DATA_DIR / "workspaces" / workspace
    cmd = f"git pull {remote} {branch}".strip()
    result = subprocess.run(cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, cwd=str(ws_dir), timeout=30)
    return {"output": result.stdout + result.stderr, "exit_code": result.returncode}


@app.post("/git/remote")
async def git_remote(request: Request):
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    workspace = body.get("workspace", "default")
    action = body.get("action", "list")
    name = body.get("name", "")
    url = body.get("url", "")
    ws_dir = DATA_DIR / "workspaces" / workspace
    if action == "add" and name and url:
        cmd = f"git remote add {name} {url}"
    elif action == "remove" and name:
        cmd = f"git remote remove {name}"
    else:
        cmd = "git remote -v"
    result = subprocess.run(cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, cwd=str(ws_dir))
    return {"output": result.stdout + result.stderr, "exit_code": result.returncode}

async def git_merge(request: Request):
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    workspace = body.get("workspace", "default")
    branch = body.get("branch", "")
    if not branch:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Branch required"}, 400)
    ws_dir = DATA_DIR / "workspaces" / workspace
    result = subprocess.run(f"git merge {branch}", shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, cwd=str(ws_dir))
    return {"output": result.stdout + result.stderr, "exit_code": result.returncode}


@app.post("/git/stash")
async def git_stash(request: Request):
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    workspace = body.get("workspace", "default")
    action = body.get("action", "save")
    ws_dir = DATA_DIR / "workspaces" / workspace
    if action == "pop":
        cmd = "git stash pop"
    elif action == "list":
        cmd = "git stash list"
    elif action == "drop":
        cmd = "git stash drop"
    else:
        cmd = "git stash"
    result = subprocess.run(cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, cwd=str(ws_dir))
    return {"output": result.stdout + result.stderr, "exit_code": result.returncode}


@app.post("/git/revert")
async def git_revert(request: Request):
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    workspace = body.get("workspace", "default")
    commit = body.get("commit", "HEAD")
    ws_dir = DATA_DIR / "workspaces" / workspace
    result = subprocess.run(f"git revert --no-edit {commit}", shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, cwd=str(ws_dir))
    return {"output": result.stdout + result.stderr, "exit_code": result.returncode}


# ─── Production: Extended Filesystem ───

@app.post("/fs/stat")
async def fs_stat(request: Request):
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    workspace = body.get("workspace", "default")
    path = body.get("path", "")
    if not path or ".." in path:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Invalid path"}, 400)
    file_path = DATA_DIR / "workspaces" / workspace / path
    if not file_path.exists():
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Not found", "path": path}, 404)
    stat = file_path.stat()
    return {
        "path": path, "size": stat.st_size, "mode": oct(stat.st_mode)[-3:],
        "is_dir": file_path.is_dir(), "is_file": file_path.is_file(),
        "modified": stat.st_mtime, "created": stat.st_ctime,
    }


@app.post("/fs/exists")
async def fs_exists(request: Request):
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    workspace = body.get("workspace", "default")
    path = body.get("path", "")
    if not path or ".." in path:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Invalid path"}, 400)
    file_path = DATA_DIR / "workspaces" / workspace / path
    return {"exists": file_path.exists(), "path": path, "is_dir": file_path.is_dir() if file_path.exists() else False}


@app.post("/fs/touch")
async def fs_touch(request: Request):
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    workspace = body.get("workspace", "default")
    path = body.get("path", "")
    if not path or ".." in path:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Invalid path"}, 400)
    file_path = DATA_DIR / "workspaces" / workspace / path
    file_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    file_path.touch()
    return {"status": "touched", "path": path}


@app.get("/fs/download")
async def fs_download(workspace: str = "default", path: str = ""):
    if not path or ".." in path:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Invalid path"}, 400)
    file_path = DATA_DIR / "workspaces" / workspace / path
    if not file_path.exists() or not file_path.is_file():
        return JSONResponse({"error": "File not found"}, 404)
    return FileResponse(str(file_path), filename=file_path.name)


@app.post("/fs/upload")
async def fs_upload(workspace: str = "default", path: str = "", file: UploadFile = File(...)):
    if not path or ".." in path:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Invalid path"}, 400)
    file_path = DATA_DIR / "workspaces" / workspace / path
    file_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    content = await file.read()
    file_path.write_bytes(content)
    return {"status": "uploaded", "path": path, "size": len(content)}


# ─── Production: Background Processes ───

_bg_processes: dict[str, dict] = {}


@app.post("/proc/background")
async def proc_background(request: Request):
    """Start a background process (long-running). Returns process ID for polling."""
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    command = body.get("command", "")
    workspace = body.get("workspace", "default")
    if not command:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "No command provided"}, 400)
    if _is_command_blocked(command):
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Command blocked by safety policy"}, 403)
    ws_dir = DATA_DIR / "workspaces" / workspace
    ws_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    proc_id = f"bg_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}"
    proc = subprocess.Popen(
        command, shell=True, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
        text=True, cwd=str(ws_dir)
    )
    _bg_processes[proc_id] = {
        "pid": proc.pid,
        "command": command,
        "process": proc,
        "started_at": time.time(),
        "status": "running",
        "output": "",
    }
    return {"proc_id": proc_id, "pid": proc.pid, "command": command, "status": "running"}


@app.get("/proc/{proc_id}/output")
async def proc_bg_output(proc_id: str):
    if proc_id not in _bg_processes:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Process not found"}, 404)
    entry = _bg_processes[proc_id]
    proc = entry["process"]
    try:
        output = proc.stdout.read() if proc.stdout else ""
        entry["output"] = output
    except Exception:
        pass
    return {
        "proc_id": proc_id,
        "output": entry["output"][-50000:],
        "status": entry["status"],
        "pid": entry["pid"],
        "elapsed": round(time.time() - entry["started_at"], 2),
    }


@app.get("/proc/{proc_id}/status")
async def proc_bg_status(proc_id: str):
    if proc_id not in _bg_processes:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Process not found"}, 404)
    entry = _bg_processes[proc_id]
    proc = entry["process"]
    poll = proc.poll()
    if poll is not None and entry["status"] == "running":
        entry["status"] = f"exited({poll})"
    return {
        "proc_id": proc_id,
        "pid": entry["pid"],
        "status": entry["status"],
        "command": entry["command"],
        "elapsed": round(time.time() - entry["started_at"], 2),
    }


@app.post("/proc/{proc_id}/kill")
async def proc_bg_kill(proc_id: str):
    if proc_id not in _bg_processes:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Process not found"}, 404)
    entry = _bg_processes[proc_id]
    proc = entry["process"]
    if entry["status"] == "running":
        proc.kill()
        entry["status"] = "killed"
    return {"proc_id": proc_id, "status": entry["status"]}


# ─── Production: Test Runner ───

@app.post("/test/run")
async def test_run(request: Request):
    """Run test suites in a workspace. Auto-detects pytest, unittest, or Rscript."""
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    workspace = body.get("workspace", "default")
    framework = body.get("framework", "auto")
    path = body.get("path", "")
    ws_dir = DATA_DIR / "workspaces" / workspace
    if not ws_dir.exists():
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Workspace not found"}, 404)

    if framework == "auto":
        if (ws_dir / "pytest.ini").exists() or any(ws_dir.glob("test_*.py")) or any(ws_dir.glob("*_test.py")):
            framework = "pytest"
        elif any(ws_dir.glob("test*.R")):
            framework = "rscript"
        elif any(ws_dir.glob("test_*.rb")):
            framework = "ruby"
        elif any(ws_dir.glob("*_test.go")) or any(ws_dir.glob("test_*.go")):
            framework = "go"
        else:
            framework = "unittest"

    cmds = {
        "pytest": f"python3 -m pytest {path} -v 2>&1",
        "unittest": f"python3 -m unittest discover {path} -v 2>&1",
        "rscript": f"Rscript test*.R 2>&1",
        "ruby": f"ruby test_*.rb 2>&1",
        "go": f"go test ./... -v 2>&1",
        "node": f"npm test 2>&1",
    }
    cmd = cmds.get(framework, cmds["unittest"])
    result = subprocess.run(cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60, cwd=str(ws_dir))
    return {
        "framework": framework,
        "output": (result.stdout + result.stderr)[-50000:],
        "exit_code": result.returncode,
        "passed": result.returncode == 0,
    }


# ─── Production: Code Snippets ───

@app.post("/snippet/save")
async def snippet_save(request: Request):
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    name = body.get("name", "")
    code = body.get("code", "")
    language = body.get("language", "python")
    if not name or not code:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Name and code required"}, 400)
    snippet_dir = DATA_DIR / "snippets"
    snippet_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    safe_name = name.replace("/", "_").replace(" ", "_")
    meta = {"name": name, "language": language, "code": code, "saved_at": time.time()}
    (snippet_dir / f"{safe_name}.json").write_text(json.dumps(meta, indent=2))
    return {"status": "saved", "name": name, "language": language}


@app.get("/snippet/list")
async def snippet_list():
    snippet_dir = DATA_DIR / "snippets"
    if not snippet_dir.exists():
        return {"snippets": []}
    snippets = []
    for f in snippet_dir.glob("*.json"):
        try:
            data = json.loads(f.read_text())
            snippets.append({"name": data["name"], "language": data["language"], "saved_at": data["saved_at"]})
        except Exception:
            pass
    return {"snippets": snippets}


@app.get("/snippet/{name}")
async def snippet_load(name: str):
    snippet_dir = DATA_DIR / "snippets"
    safe_name = name.replace("/", "_").replace(" ", "_")
    filepath = snippet_dir / f"{safe_name}.json"
    if not filepath.exists():
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Snippet not found"}, 404)
    return json.loads(filepath.read_text())

    "python-cli": {"files": {"main.py": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\nimport sys\n\ndef main():\n    print('Hello from CLI')\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n", "requirements.txt": "", "README.md": "# Python CLI Project\n"}},

    "r-script": {"files": {"main.R": "# R Project\nprint('Hello from R')\n", "README.md": "# R Project\n"}},
    "node-api": {"files": {"index.js": "const http = require('http');\nconst server = http.createServer((req, res) => {\n  res.writeHead(200, {'Content-Type': 'application/json'});\n  res.end(JSON.stringify({status: 'ok'}));\n});\nserver.listen(3000);\n", "package.json": '{"name": "node-api", "version": "1.0.0"}'}},
    "go-cli": {"files": {"main.go": "package main\n\nimport \"fmt\"\n\nfunc main() {\n    fmt.Println(\"Hello from Go\")\n}\n", "go.mod": "module cli\n\ngo 1.21\n"}},
    "rust-cli": {"files": {"main.rs": "fn main() {\n    println!(\"Hello from Rust\");\n}\n", "Cargo.toml": "[package]\nname = \"cli\"\nversion = \"0.1.0\"\nedition = \"2021\"\n"}},
    "c-project": {"files": {"main.c": "#include <stdio.h>\nint main() {\n    printf(\"Hello from C\\n\");\n    return 0;\n}\n", "Makefile": "all: main\nmain: main.c\n\tgcc -o main main.c\n"}},
    "cpp-project": {"files": {"main.cpp": "#include <iostream>\nint main() {\n    std::cout << \"Hello from C++\" << std::endl;\n    return 0;\n}\n", "Makefile": "all: main\nmain: main.cpp\n\tg++ -o main main.cpp\n"}},
}


@app.get("/template/list")
async def template_list():
    return {"templates": list(TEMPLATES.keys())}


@app.post("/template/apply")
async def template_apply(request: Request):
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    template = body.get("template", "")
    workspace = body.get("workspace", "default")
    if template not in TEMPLATES:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Template not found", "available": list(TEMPLATES.keys())}, 404)
    ws_dir = DATA_DIR / "workspaces" / workspace
    ws_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    files = TEMPLATES[template]["files"]
    for filename, content in files.items():
        filepath = ws_dir / filename
        filepath.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        filepath.write_text(content)
    return {"status": "applied", "template": template, "workspace": workspace, "files": list(files.keys())}


# ─── Production: Async Task Queue ───

_task_queue: dict[str, dict] = {}


@app.post("/queue/task")
async def queue_task(request: Request):
    """Submit an async task. Returns task_id for polling."""
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    command = body.get("command", "")
    workspace = body.get("workspace", "default")
    if not command:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "No command provided"}, 400)
    if _is_command_blocked(command):
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Command blocked"}, 403)
    task_id = f"task_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}"
    _task_queue[task_id] = {
        "command": command,
        "workspace": workspace,
        "status": "pending",
        "output": "",
        "exit_code": None,
        "created_at": time.time(),
        "completed_at": None,
    }

    def _run_task():
        ws_dir = DATA_DIR / "workspaces" / workspace
        ws_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        _task_queue[task_id]["status"] = "running"
        try:
            result = subprocess.run(command, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=120, cwd=str(ws_dir))
            _task_queue[task_id]["output"] = (result.stdout + result.stderr)[-50000:]
            _task_queue[task_id]["exit_code"] = result.returncode
            _task_queue[task_id]["status"] = "completed"
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            _task_queue[task_id]["status"] = "timeout"
            _task_queue[task_id]["output"] = "Command timed out (120s)"
        except Exception as e:
            _task_queue[task_id]["status"] = "error"
            _task_queue[task_id]["output"] = str(e)
        _task_queue[task_id]["completed_at"] = time.time()

    thread = threading.Thread(target=_run_task, daemon=True)
    thread.start()
    return {"task_id": task_id, "status": "pending", "command": command}


@app.get("/queue/{task_id}/status")
async def queue_status(task_id: str):
    if task_id not in _task_queue:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Task not found"}, 404)
    t = _task_queue[task_id]
    return {
        "task_id": task_id,
        "status": t["status"],
        "exit_code": t["exit_code"],
        "elapsed": round(time.time() - t["created_at"], 2),
    }


@app.get("/queue/{task_id}/result")
async def queue_result(task_id: str):
    if task_id not in _task_queue:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Task not found"}, 404)
    t = _task_queue[task_id]
    return {
        "task_id": task_id,
        "status": t["status"],
        "output": t["output"],
        "exit_code": t["exit_code"],
        "elapsed": round(time.time() - t["created_at"], 2),
        "completed": t["status"] in ("completed", "timeout", "error"),
    }


@app.get("/queue/list")
async def queue_list():
    return {
        "tasks": [
            {"task_id": tid, "status": t["status"], "command": t["command"][:100], "created_at": t["created_at"]}
            for tid, t in _task_queue.items()
        ]
    }


# ─── Production: Environment Management ───

_runtime_env: dict = {}


@app.post("/env/set")
async def env_set(request: Request):
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    key = body.get("key", "")
    value = body.get("value", "")
    if not key:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Key required"}, 400)
    safe_keys_block = ["EXEC_AUTH_TOKEN", "HF_TOKEN", "OPENAI_API_KEY", "GROQ_API_KEY", "SECRET", "PASSWORD", "TOKEN"]
    if any(s in key.upper() for s in safe_keys_block):
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Cannot set secret keys"}, 403)
    _runtime_env[key] = value
    os.environ[key] = value
    return {"status": "set", "key": key}


@app.post("/env/unset")
async def env_unset(request: Request):
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    key = body.get("key", "")
    if not key:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Key required"}, 400)
    _runtime_env.pop(key, None)
    os.environ.pop(key, None)
    return {"status": "unset", "key": key}


# ─── Production: Crypto Utilities ───

@app.post("/crypto/hash")
async def crypto_hash(request: Request):
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    data = body.get("data", "")
    algorithm = body.get("algorithm", "sha256")
    if algorithm not in ("sha256", "sha512", "sha1", "md5"):
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Unsupported algorithm"}, 400)
    h = hashlib.new(algorithm)
    h.update(data.encode())
    return {"hash": h.hexdigest(), "algorithm": algorithm, "input_length": len(data)}


@app.post("/crypto/encode")
async def crypto_encode(request: Request):
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    data = body.get("data", "")
    encoding = body.get("encoding", "base64")
    if encoding == "base64":
        return {"encoded": base64.b64encode(data.encode()).decode(), "encoding": encoding}
    elif encoding == "hex":
        return {"encoded": data.encode().hex(), "encoding": encoding}
    elif encoding == "url":
        import urllib.parse
        return {"encoded": urllib.parse.quote(data), "encoding": encoding}
    return JSONResponse({"error": "Unsupported encoding"}, 400)


@app.post("/crypto/decode")
async def crypto_decode(request: Request):
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    data = body.get("data", "")
    encoding = body.get("encoding", "base64")
    try:
        if encoding == "base64":
            return {"decoded": base64.b64decode(data).decode("utf-8", errors="replace"), "encoding": encoding}
        elif encoding == "hex":
            return {"decoded": bytes.fromhex(data).decode("utf-8", errors="replace"), "encoding": encoding}
        elif encoding == "url":
            import urllib.parse
            return {"decoded": urllib.parse.unquote(data), "encoding": encoding}
    except Exception as e:
        return JSONResponse({"error": str(e)}, 400)
    return JSONResponse({"error": "Unsupported encoding"}, 400)


# ─── Production: Data Conversion ───

@app.post("/data/convert")
async def data_convert(request: Request):
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    data = body.get("data", "")
    from_format = body.get("from", "json").lower()
    to_format = body.get("to", "yaml").lower()

    if from_format == to_format:
        return {"result": data, "from": from_format, "to": to_format}

    try:
        if from_format == "json":
            parsed = json.loads(data)
        elif from_format == "csv":
            import csv
            import io
            reader = csv.DictReader(io.StringIO(data))
            parsed = list(reader)
        elif from_format == "yaml":
            try:
                import yaml
                parsed = yaml.safe_load(data)
            except ImportError:
                return JSONResponse({"error": "YAML support not installed. Run: pip install pyyaml"}, 400)
        else:
            return JSONResponse({"error": f"Unsupported source format: {from_format}"}, 400)

        if to_format == "json":
            result = json.dumps(parsed, indent=2)
        elif to_format == "csv":
            import csv
            import io
            buf = io.StringIO()
            if isinstance(parsed, list) and parsed:
                writer = csv.DictWriter(buf, fieldnames=parsed[0].keys())
                writer.writeheader()
                writer.writerows(parsed)
            result = buf.getvalue()
        elif to_format == "yaml":
            try:
                import yaml
                result = yaml.dump(parsed, default_flow_style=False)
            except ImportError:
                return JSONResponse({"error": "YAML support not installed"}, 400)
        else:
            return JSONResponse({"error": f"Unsupported target format: {to_format}"}, 400)

        return {"result": result, "from": from_format, "to": to_format}
    except Exception as e:
        return JSONResponse({"error": str(e)}, 400)


# ─── Production: AI Direct Endpoints ───

@app.post("/ai/complete")
async def ai_complete(request: Request):
    """Direct LLM completion using the configured provider."""
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    prompt = body.get("prompt", "")
    max_tokens = min(int(body.get("max_tokens", 2000)), 4000)
    if not prompt:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Prompt required"}, 400)

    provider = os.getenv("PROVIDER", "huggingface").lower().strip()
    try:
        if provider == "huggingface":
            from huggingface_hub import InferenceClient
            token = os.getenv("HF_TOKEN", "")
            client = InferenceClient(token=token)
            model = os.getenv("HF_TEXT_MODEL", "Qwen/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct")
            response = client.chat_completion(
                model=model,
                messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
                max_tokens=max_tokens,
            )
            return {"completion": response.choices[0].message.content, "model": model, "provider": provider}
        elif provider == "groq":
            from src.builder_llm import call_grok
            output = call_grok(prompt)
            return {"completion": output, "provider": provider}
        else:
            return JSONResponse({"error": f"Provider {provider} not configured for direct completion"}, 400)
    except Exception as e:
        return JSONResponse({"error": str(e)}, 500)


@app.post("/ai/chat")
async def ai_chat(request: Request):
    """Multi-turn chat with LLM."""
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    messages = body.get("messages", [])
    if not messages:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Messages required"}, 400)

    provider = os.getenv("PROVIDER", "huggingface").lower().strip()
    try:
        if provider == "huggingface":
            from huggingface_hub import InferenceClient
            token = os.getenv("HF_TOKEN", "")
            client = InferenceClient(token=token)
            model = os.getenv("HF_TEXT_MODEL", "Qwen/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct")
            response = client.chat_completion(
                model=model,
                messages=messages,
                max_tokens=2000,
            )
            return {"response": response.choices[0].message.content, "model": model, "provider": provider}
        else:
            prompt = "\n".join(f"{m['role']}: {m['content']}" for m in messages)
            from src.builder_llm import call_grok
            output = call_grok(prompt)
            return {"response": output, "provider": provider}
    except Exception as e:
        return JSONResponse({"error": str(e)}, 500)


# ─── Production: Audit Log ───

_audit_log: list[dict] = []
_audit_chain_hash = "0" * 64


def _add_audit_entry(action: str, details: dict):
    global _audit_chain_hash
    entry = {
        "id": uuid.uuid4().hex,
        "timestamp": time.time(),
        "action": action,
        "details": details,
        "prev_hash": _audit_chain_hash,
    }
    entry_str = json.dumps(entry, sort_keys=True)
    entry["hash"] = hashlib.sha256(entry_str.encode()).hexdigest()
    _audit_chain_hash = entry["hash"]
    _audit_log.append(entry)
    if len(_audit_log) > 500:
        _audit_log.pop(0)


@app.get("/audit/log")
async def audit_log(limit: int = 50):
    return {
        "entries": _audit_log[-limit:],
        "total": len(_audit_log),
        "chain_head": _audit_chain_hash,
    }


@app.get("/audit/verify")
async def audit_verify():
    prev = "0" * 64
    for i, entry in enumerate(_audit_log):
        expected = hashlib.sha256(
            json.dumps({k: v for k, v in entry.items() if k != "hash"}, sort_keys=True).encode()
        ).hexdigest()
        if entry["prev_hash"] != prev:
            return {"valid": False, "broken_at": i, "entry_id": entry["id"]}
        prev = entry["hash"]
    return {"valid": True, "entries": len(_audit_log), "chain_head": _audit_chain_hash}


# ─── Production: Backup/Restore ───

@app.post("/backup/create")
async def backup_create(request: Request):
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    workspace = body.get("workspace", "default")
    ws_dir = DATA_DIR / "workspaces" / workspace
    if not ws_dir.exists():
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Workspace not found"}, 404)
    import shutil
    backup_dir = DATA_DIR / "backups"
    backup_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    backup_name = f"{workspace}_{int(time.time())}"
    backup_path = backup_dir / f"{backup_name}.tar.gz"
    shutil.make_archive(str(backup_dir / backup_name), "gztar", str(ws_dir))
    size = backup_path.stat().st_size
    _add_audit_entry("backup_create", {"workspace": workspace, "backup": backup_name, "size": size})
    return {"status": "created", "backup": backup_name, "size_bytes": size, "path": str(backup_path)}


@app.post("/backup/restore")
async def backup_restore(request: Request):
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    backup_name = body.get("backup", "")
    workspace = body.get("workspace", "default")
    if not backup_name:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Backup name required"}, 400)
    backup_path = DATA_DIR / "backups" / f"{backup_name}.tar.gz"
    if not backup_path.exists():
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Backup not found"}, 404)
    import shutil
    ws_dir = DATA_DIR / "workspaces" / workspace
    if ws_dir.exists():
        shutil.rmtree(ws_dir)
    ws_dir.mkdir(parents=True)
    shutil.unpack_archive(str(backup_path), str(ws_dir))
    _add_audit_entry("backup_restore", {"workspace": workspace, "backup": backup_name})
    return {"status": "restored", "workspace": workspace, "backup": backup_name}


@app.get("/backup/list")
async def backup_list():
    backup_dir = DATA_DIR / "backups"
    if not backup_dir.exists():
        return {"backups": []}
    backups = []
    for f in backup_dir.glob("*.tar.gz"):
        backups.append({"name": f.stem, "size_bytes": f.stat().st_size, "created": f.stat().st_mtime})
    return {"backups": backups}


# ─── Production: Network Utilities (Safe) ───

@app.post("/net/check")
async def net_check(request: Request):
    """Check if a host:port is reachable (TCP connect only, no raw sockets)."""
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    host = body.get("host", "")
    port = int(body.get("port", 80))
    timeout = min(float(body.get("timeout", 5)), 10)
    if not host:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Host required"}, 400)
    import socket
    try:
        sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
        sock.settimeout(timeout)
        result = sock.connect_ex((host, port))
        sock.close()
        return {"host": host, "port": port, "reachable": result == 0, "error_code": result}
    except Exception as e:
        return {"host": host, "port": port, "reachable": False, "error": str(e)}


@app.post("/net/dns")
async def net_dns(request: Request):
    """DNS lookup for a hostname."""
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    hostname = body.get("hostname", "")
    if not hostname:
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Hostname required"}, 400)
    import socket
    try:
        addr = socket.getaddrinfo(hostname, None)
        results = [{"family": a[0], "addr": a[4][0]} for a in addr]
        return {"hostname": hostname, "addresses": results}
    except Exception as e:
        return JSONResponse({"error": str(e)}, 400)


@app.post("/net/http")
async def net_http(request: Request):
    """Make an HTTP request (GET/POST) to a URL."""
    body = {}
    try:
        body = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        pass
    url = body.get("url", "")
    method = body.get("method", "GET").upper()
    headers = body.get("headers", {})
    data = body.get("data", "")
    if not url or not url.startswith(("http://", "https://")):
        return JSONResponse({"error": "Valid URL required"}, 400)
    import urllib.request
    try:
        req_data = data.encode() if data else None
        req = urllib.request.Request(url, data=req_data, headers=headers, method=method)
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=15) as resp:
            content = resp.read(500000).decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
            return {"status": resp.status, "headers": dict(resp.headers), "body": content[:100000]}
    except Exception as e:
        return JSONResponse({"error": str(e)}, 500)

<button class="btn" onclick="loadFile()">📂 Load</button>
<button class="btn" onclick="listFiles()">📁 Files</button>

<div class="editor">
<div class="code-area">
<textarea id="code" placeholder="# Write code here...">print("Hello from CSC Engine IDE!")
</textarea>
<div class="terminal">
<div class="terminal-header">Terminal Output</div>
<div class="terminal-output" id="output"></div>
<div class="terminal-input">
<input id="cmd" placeholder="Shell command..." onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter')runCmd()">
</div>
</div>

<script>
const API = window.location.origin;
const output = document.getElementById('output');
const code = document.getElementById('code');
const lang = document.getElementById('lang');
function log(msg) { output.textContent += msg + '\\n'; output.scrollTop = output.scrollHeight; }
async function runCode() {
  log('▶ Running ' + lang.value + '...');
  const r = await fetch(API + '/lang/execute', {method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
    body: JSON.stringify({code: code.value, language: lang.value})});
  const d = await r.json();
  if (d.output) log(d.output);
  if (d.exit_code !== undefined) log('Exit: ' + d.exit_code);
}
async function runCmd() {
  const cmd = document.getElementById('cmd').value;
  document.getElementById('cmd').value = '';
  log('$ ' + cmd);
  const r = await fetch(API + '/exec', {method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
    body: JSON.stringify({command: cmd})});
  const d = await r.json();
  if (d.output) log(d.output);
}
async function saveFile() {
  const path = prompt('File path:', 'main.py');
  if (!path) return;
  await fetch(API + '/files/write', {method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
    body: JSON.stringify({path, content: code.value})});
  log('💾 Saved ' + path);
}
async function loadFile() {
  const path = prompt('File path:', 'main.py');
  if (!path) return;
  const r = await fetch(API + '/files/read', {method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
    body: JSON.stringify({path})});
  const d = await r.json();
  if (d.content) { code.value = d.content; log('📂 Loaded ' + path); }
}
async function listFiles() {
  const r = await fetch(API + '/files/list', {method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body: '{}'});
  const d = await r.json();
  log('📁 Files:'); (d.files||[]).forEach(f => log('  ' + f.path + ' (' + f.size + 'B)'));
}
async function fetchMetrics() {
  const r = await fetch(API + '/metrics'); const d = await r.json();
  log('📊 Requests: ' + d.requests_total + ' | Errors: ' + d.errors_total + ' | Uptime: ' + d.uptime_seconds + 's');
}
async function fetchHealth() {
  const r = await fetch(API + '/health/detailed'); const d = await r.json();
  log('🏥 Status: ' + d.status); Object.entries(d.checks).forEach(([k,v]) => log('  ' + k + ': ' + (v.status||v.disk)));
}
async function fetchLangs() {
  const r = await fetch(API + '/lang/list'); const d = await r.json();
  log('🔤 Languages (' + d.total + '):'); Object.entries(d.languages).forEach(([k,v]) => {
    if (v.available) log('  ✓ ' + k + ' → ' + v.path);
  });
}
async function fetchSysInfo() {
  const r = await fetch(API + '/sys/info'); const d = await r.json();
  log('💻 OS: ' + d.os + ' | CPU: ' + d.cpu_count + ' | Python: ' + d.python_version + ' | Provider: ' + d.provider);
}
log('CSC Engine IDE ready. Press ▶ Run or type a shell command below.');
fetch(API + '/health').then(r => r.json()).then(d => {
  document.getElementById('status').textContent = '● ' + d.provider + ' | sessions: ' + d.sessions_active;
});
</script>
</body>
</html>"""
    return HTMLResponse(ide_html)

async def gpt_actions_schema():
    """ChatGPT Custom GPT Actions schema — OpenAPI 3.1 with requestBody for POST endpoints."""
    return {
        "openapi": "3.1.0",

            <div class="ts-row"><span>Status</span><span class="ts-val" id="tsStatus">—</span></div>
            <div class="ts-row"><span>Uptime</span><span class="ts-val" id="tsUptime">—</span></div>
            <div class="ts-row"><span>Lines</span><span class="ts-val" id="tsLines">—</span></div>
            <div class="ts-row"><span>Last cmd</span><span class="ts-val" id="tsLastCmd">—</span></div>
            <div style="margin-top:12px"></div>
            <h4>Quick Actions</h4>
            <button class="ts-btn" onclick="termExec('ls -la')">ls -la</button>
            <button class="ts-btn" onclick="termExec('python3 --version')">python3 --version</button>
            <button class="ts-btn" onclick="termExec('whoami && hostname')">whoami</button>

            <button class="ts-btn" onclick="termExec('df -h /')">df -h</button>
            <button class="ts-btn" onclick="termExec('free -m')">free -m</button>
            <button class="ts-btn" onclick="pollTerminalOutput()">Refresh Output</button>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="terminal-history" id="termHistoryPanel" style="display:none">
          <h4>Command History (click to rerun)</h4>
          <div id="termHistoryList"></div>
        </div>
        <div class="terminal-input-row">
          <span class="prompt">csc@kali:~$</span>
          <input class="terminal-input" id="termInput" placeholder="Type a command and press Enter..."
            onkeydown="handleTermInput(event)" disabled />

    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- ═══════════════ IDE VIEW ═══════════════ -->

    <input type="text" id="cmdInput" placeholder="Search commands..." oninput="filterCmds()"
